  │  87 +                                                                  │
  │  88 + Follow `src/domains/d_aerospace/invariants.py`:                  │
  │  89 +                                                                  │
  │  90 + ```python                                                        │
  │  91 + """Domain invariant checks — Yeshua Standard.                    │
  │  92 +                                                                  │
  │  93 + Each function returns Tuple[bool, ProofObject].                  │
  │  94 + No assert statements. No float values — Fraction only.           │
  │  95 + """                                                              │
  │  96 +                                                                  │
  │  97 + from fractions import Fraction                                   │
  │  98 + from typing import Tuple                                         │
  │  99 + from axioms.logic import ProofObject                             │
  │ 100 +                                                                  │
  │ 101 + def check_invariant() -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:               │
  │ 102 +     """                                                          │
  │ 103 +     Invariant: Description of what must hold.                    │
  │ 104 +                                                                  │
  │ 105 +     Standard: Real regulatory standard (e.g., "DO-178C", "14 CF… │
  │ 106 +     Falsifies if: Condition that would violate the invariant.    │
  │ 107 +                                                                  │
  │ 108 +     Returns:                                                     │
  │ 109 +         Tuple of (success: bool, proof: ProofObject)             │
  │ 110 +     """                                                          │
  │ 111 +     # Use Fraction for all calculations                          │
  │ 112 +     value = Fraction(100) / Fraction(3)                          │
  │ 113 +                                                                  │
  │ 114 +     success = value == Fraction(100, 3)                          │
  │ 115 +                                                                  │
  │ 116 +     proof = ProofObject(                                         │
  │ 117 +         rule="InvariantCheckRule",                               │
  │ 118 +         premises=[f"value = {value}"],                           │
  │ 119 +         conclusion=(                                             │
  │ 120 +             "Invariant holds per Standard"                       │
  │ 121 +             if success else "FAIL: Invariant violated"           │
  │ 122 +         ),                                                       │
  │ 123 +     )                                                            │
  │ 124 +     return success, proof                                        │
  │ 125 + ```                                                              │
